# PR #11141: Implement KEP-9988 (MultiKueue Manager Quota Automation), Alpha1 stage

**Summary**: Implement KEP-9988 (MultiKueue Manager Quota Automation), Alpha1 stage

**Sources**: https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11141

**Last updated**: 2026-05-20T09:38:00Z

---

## Description

#### What type of PR is this?

/kind feature
/area multikueue

#### What this PR does / why we need it:

It adds a feature which automatically updates MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue quotas, based on summing up worker-side quotas.

#### Which issue(s) this PR fixes:

Fixes #9988 _but only in Alpha1 scope_ - I'm not sure how to track this.

#### Special notes for your reviewer:

* Heavily vibe-coded, but also heavily supervised.

* **Informers** for remote clusters had to be introduced to avoid triggering the logic by irrelevant remote CQ changes.
  (Plain "Watch" gives no insight into the previous state, hence no insight into "what has changed").
  Given that, I went a step further and created `SelectivelyCachingClient` to achieve [this follow-up](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/tree/main/keps/9988-multikueue-manager-quota-automation#use-cached-remote-clients-for-low-volume-resource-kinds) from the KEP.
  As a result, all **remote** `Get`s and `List`s on CQs and LQs are **cached**, which optimizes the feature even further.

* The test coverage is not yet in the full KEP-planned scope.
  However, I think it's already decent, so I'm sending it for review now.
  More tests can be added in follow-up PRs. (Or, if anyone insists, I can add them also in this PR).
  * Tests currently provided:
    - unit tests for various exit paths from `Reconcile()`
    - unit tests for triggering MK CQ Reconciler by AC / MKConfig / MKCluster changes
    - integration tests verifying MK CQ Reconciliation on remote CQ/LQ changes
    - integration tests verifying that MK CQ Reconciler is inactive on FG disabled
    - basic e2e test "it essentially works"
  * Tests still TODO:
    - e2e tests besides the minimal scope (see [KEP test plan](https://github.com/olekzabl/kueue/blob/d3f1456215d5007321f511254f38ba49a409da1e/keps/9988-multikueue-manager-quota-automation/README.md#e2e-tests))
 
#### Does this PR introduce a user-facing change?
```release-note
MultiKueue: Keep manager ClusterQueue quota equal to the sum of workers' quotas. This is enabled by the `MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation` feature gate, and by `.QuotaManagement` in MultiKueueConfig.
```

### Comment by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) — 2026-05-19T09:15:50Z

@mimowo @kshalot PTAL

FWIW **summary of main changes** since our yesterday's discussion (specifically since commit [ec6313e](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11141/commits/ec6313e84a7337729a6e3ae5224634714879f31d)):

1. Made `rc.client` smarter (see [`remote_client.go`](https://github.com/olekzabl/kueue/blob/df78244250c419ccb11ec01ab74c1d95bee3349e/pkg/controller/admissionchecks/multikueue/remote_client.go)) - so that it:
   - Uses informer cache for [past-state-aware watching](https://github.com/olekzabl/kueue/blob/df78244250c419ccb11ec01ab74c1d95bee3349e/pkg/controller/admissionchecks/multikueue/multikueuecluster.go#L408-L414), but [also `Get` and `List` calls](https://github.com/olekzabl/kueue/blob/df78244250c419ccb11ec01ab74c1d95bee3349e/pkg/controller/admissionchecks/multikueue/remote_client.go#L76-L99)
   - Does that only for specified `cachedKinds` (currently: LQ & CQ); otherwise falls back to the old client.

2. Added [integration tests](https://github.com/olekzabl/kueue/blob/df78244250c419ccb11ec01ab74c1d95bee3349e/test/integration/multikueue/setup_test.go#L783-L866) to verify reaction to remote LQ / CQ changes.

3. Equipped `remoteClient` with an internal `RWMutex`; this made new integration tests happier.

4. Reverted some changes which are now not relevant (or less relevant):
   - the refactor around `startWorkloadOrJobWatcher`, `MultiKueueWatcher` (no longer relevant)
   - the `getOrList` helper (much less relevant due to caching)
   - parallelization in quota computation (much less relevant due to caching)

  Is this handling the case where a cluster queue stops being a MK cluster queue? I guess it's possible in theory.
  
  I'm thinking about this because this check could be moved to the predicates so avoid doing reconciles for every CQ in the cluster. I don't think it's a big deal though, because there aren't that many and this is an opt-in controller.

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,460 @@
+/*
+Copyright The Kubernetes Authors.
+
+Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");

+You may obtain a copy of the License at

+See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+limitations under the License.
+*/
+
+package multikueue
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"fmt"
+
+	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/equality"
+	apierrors "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
+	apimeta "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/meta"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
+	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/types"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/sets"
+	"k8s.io/client-go/util/workqueue"
+	ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/builder"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/controller"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/event"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/handler"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/reconcile"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/source"
+
+	kueue "sigs.k8s.io/kueue/apis/kueue/v1beta2"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/controller/core/indexer"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/features"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/util/admissioncheck"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/util/roletracker"
+)
+
+type CQReconciler struct {
+	client      client.Client
+	helper      *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper
+	clusters    *clustersReconciler
+	roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker
+}
+
+var _ reconcile.Reconciler = (*CQReconciler)(nil)
+
+func newCQReconciler(c client.Client, helper *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper, clusters *clustersReconciler, roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker) *CQReconciler {
+	return &CQReconciler{
+		client:      c,
+		helper:      helper,
+		clusters:    clusters,
+		roleTracker: roleTracker,
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req reconcile.Request) (reconcile.Result, error) {
+	if !features.Enabled(features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation) {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, nil
+	}
+
+	log := ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).WithValues("clusterQueue", req.Name)
+	log.V(3).Info("Reconcile ClusterQueue event received (in the MultiKueue controller)")
+
+	cq := &kueue.ClusterQueue{}
+	if err := r.client.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, cq); err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
+	}
+
+	ac, hasAC, err := r.getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx, cq)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	if !hasAC {
+		log.V(3).Info("Not a MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue, skipping reconcile.")
+		err := r.removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	log.V(2).Info("Reconciling MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue")
+
+	cfg, err := r.helper.ConfigFromRef(ctx, ac.Spec.Parameters)
+	if err != nil {
+		if apierrors.IsNotFound(err) {
+			err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "The referenced MultiKueueConfig was not found.")
+		}
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if cfg.Spec.QuotaAutomation != nil && cfg.Spec.QuotaAutomation.Mode == kueue.QuotaAutomationManual {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "NotRequested", "MultiKueue manager quota automation has not been requested.")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 || len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "manager-side ClusterQueue must have exactly one ResourceFlavor")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	singleFlavor := &cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0]
+
+	aggregatedQuotas, err := r.aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx, cq, cfg)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	covered := sets.New(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources...)
+	remoteResources := sets.KeySet(aggregatedQuotas)
+	missingResources := remoteResources.Difference(covered)
+	if missingResources.Len() > 0 {
+		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("manager-side coveredResources is missing resources configured on workers: %v", sets.List(missingResources))
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", errMsg)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	var newResources []kueue.ResourceQuota
+	for _, resName := range cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources {
+		newResources = append(newResources, kueue.ResourceQuota{
+			Name:         resName,
+			NominalQuota: aggregatedQuotas[resName],
+		})
+	}
+
+	if !equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(singleFlavor.Resources, newResources) {
+		singleFlavor.Resources = newResources
+		if err := r.client.Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+			return reconcile.Result{}, fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue nominal quotas: %w", err)
+		}
+	}
+
+	err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionTrue, "QuotaAutomated", "ClusterQueue quota is automatically managed based on MultiKueue workers.")
+	return reconcile.Result{}, err
+}
+

+	if cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy == nil {

+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey: kueue.MultiKueueControllerName}); err != nil {

+
+	cqACNames := sets.New[string]()

+		cqACNames.Insert(string(rule.Name))

+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {

+		}
+	}
+
+	return nil, false, nil
+}
```

  I think handling `rc.connecting` is still missing:
  
  > To cover for that, we could increase the manager-side quota by the sum of requests of all workloads which are admitted on the manager but their assigned worker clusters have remote clients which are currently `.connecting`. (Notably, all this info is available on the manager side, without querying the workers).


  I remember a discussion in the KEP about the where this API should be and some related things like handling constraints that were not met etc.
  
  So a quick question for a sanity check - do we even need to commit to this minimal Manual/Automated API for the alpha? We could just automate for every MK CQ when the fg is enabled to gather initial feedback.

### Review by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) (commented) — 2026-05-12T13:56:31Z

- **apis/kueue/v1beta2/multikueue_types.go:163** by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) — 2026-05-12T13:56:31Z
```diff

+	// +optional
+	QuotaAutomation *QuotaAutomation `json:"quotaAutomation,omitempty"`
```

  FG is cluster-wide; this flip is configurable per CQ.
  I think we want to have it ultimately, so exposing in Alpha enables gathering early feedback.
  
  While the KEP speculates that we may move it (from MKConfig to CQSpec), the field name & values are considered stabilized (once KEP is approved :), so it's even more worth exposing for feedback.

```diff
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/equality"
+	apierrors "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
+	apimeta "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/meta"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
+	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/types"

+	"k8s.io/client-go/util/workqueue"
+	ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/builder"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/controller"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/event"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/handler"

+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/reconcile"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/source"
+
+	kueue "sigs.k8s.io/kueue/apis/kueue/v1beta2"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/controller/core/indexer"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/features"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/util/admissioncheck"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/util/roletracker"
+)
+
+type CQReconciler struct {
+	client      client.Client
+	helper      *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper
+	clusters    *clustersReconciler
+	roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker
+}
+
+var _ reconcile.Reconciler = (*CQReconciler)(nil)
+
+func newCQReconciler(c client.Client, helper *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper, clusters *clustersReconciler, roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker) *CQReconciler {
+	return &CQReconciler{
+		client:      c,
+		helper:      helper,
+		clusters:    clusters,
+		roleTracker: roleTracker,
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req reconcile.Request) (reconcile.Result, error) {
+	if !features.Enabled(features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation) {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, nil
+	}
+
+	log := ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).WithValues("clusterQueue", req.Name)
+	log.V(3).Info("Reconcile ClusterQueue event received (in the MultiKueue controller)")
+
+	cq := &kueue.ClusterQueue{}
+	if err := r.client.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, cq); err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
+	}
+
+	ac, hasAC, err := r.getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx, cq)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	if !hasAC {
+		log.V(3).Info("Not a MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue, skipping reconcile.")
+		err := r.removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	log.V(2).Info("Reconciling MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue")
+
+	cfg, err := r.helper.ConfigFromRef(ctx, ac.Spec.Parameters)
+	if err != nil {
+		if apierrors.IsNotFound(err) {
+			err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "The referenced MultiKueueConfig was not found.")
+		}
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if cfg.Spec.QuotaAutomation != nil && cfg.Spec.QuotaAutomation.Mode == kueue.QuotaAutomationManual {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "NotRequested", "MultiKueue manager quota automation has not been requested.")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 || len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "manager-side ClusterQueue must have exactly one ResourceFlavor")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	singleFlavor := &cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0]
+
+	aggregatedQuotas, err := r.aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx, cq, cfg)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	covered := sets.New(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources...)
+	remoteResources := sets.KeySet(aggregatedQuotas)
+	missingResources := remoteResources.Difference(covered)
+	if missingResources.Len() > 0 {
+		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("manager-side coveredResources is missing resources configured on workers: %v", sets.List(missingResources))
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", errMsg)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	var newResources []kueue.ResourceQuota
+	for _, resName := range cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources {
+		newResources = append(newResources, kueue.ResourceQuota{
+			Name:         resName,
+			NominalQuota: aggregatedQuotas[resName],
+		})
+	}
+
+	if !equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(singleFlavor.Resources, newResources) {
+		singleFlavor.Resources = newResources
+		if err := r.client.Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+			return reconcile.Result{}, fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue nominal quotas: %w", err)
+		}
+	}
+
+	err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionTrue, "QuotaAutomated", "ClusterQueue quota is automatically managed based on MultiKueue workers.")
+	return reconcile.Result{}, err
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) (*kueue.AdmissionCheck, bool, error) {
+	if cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy == nil {
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+
+	acList := &kueue.AdmissionCheckList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey: kueue.MultiKueueControllerName}); err != nil {
+		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("listing local AdmissionChecks: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	cqACNames := sets.New[string]()
+	for _, rule := range cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy.AdmissionChecks {
+		cqACNames.Insert(string(rule.Name))
+	}
+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {
+		if cqACNames.Has(ac.Name) {
+			return &ac, true, nil
+		}
+	}
+
+	return nil, false, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, cfg *kueue.MultiKueueConfig) (map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity, error) {
+	totals := make(map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity)
+
+	localLQs := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, localLQs, client.MatchingFields{indexer.QueueClusterQueueKey: cq.Name}); err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("listing local LocalQueues: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	lqKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+	for _, llq := range localLQs.Items {
+		lqKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: llq.Namespace, Name: llq.Name})
+	}
+
+	for _, clusterName := range cfg.Spec.Clusters {
```

  Cleaner:
  ```go
  func (rc *remoteClient) handleConnectionError(err error) (*time.Duration, error) {

      return new(retryAfter(rc.failedConnAttempts)), err
  }
  ```

  > Is this handling the case where a cluster queue stops being a MK cluster queue? I guess it's possible in theory.
  
  Yes. I just checked this in practice - you can remove the MK AC from an existing CQ.
  
  And in such case, I want the quota automation condition removed, which feels clearly not fit for a predicate.


  I considered this before, and my view was different.
  
  For me, `func (rc *remoteClient) handleConnnectionError` sounds like some "general connection error handler for the whole `remoteClient`, which tries fixing stuff, etc.". While here it's just for the internal purpose of this single method.
  
  That's why I deliberately wanted it nested.
  
  WDYT?

  Looking around, many our `Reconcile()`s have such entry logs also, typically at `V(2)`.
  (In this case, it's `V(3)` due to the initial triaging; I'll emit `V(2)` once we know it's a MK CQ).

```diff
+type CQReconciler struct {

+	helper      *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper
+	clusters    *clustersReconciler
+	roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker
+}
+
+var _ reconcile.Reconciler = (*CQReconciler)(nil)
+
+func newCQReconciler(c client.Client, helper *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper, clusters *clustersReconciler, roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker) *CQReconciler {
+	return &CQReconciler{
+		client:      c,
+		helper:      helper,
+		clusters:    clusters,
+		roleTracker: roleTracker,
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req reconcile.Request) (reconcile.Result, error) {
+	if !features.Enabled(features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation) {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, nil
+	}
```

```diff
@@ -1160,3 +1169,93 @@ var _ = ginkgo.Describe("MultiKueue with ClusterProfile", ginkgo.Label("area:mul
 		})
 	})
 })
+
+type interceptedCache struct {
+	cache.Cache
+	mu               sync.Mutex
+	requestedWatches sets.Set[string]
+}
+
+func newInterceptedCache() (*interceptedCache, cache.NewCacheFunc) {
+	ic := &interceptedCache{
+		requestedWatches: sets.New[string](),
+	}
+	fn := func(config *rest.Config, opts cache.Options) (cache.Cache, error) {
+		baseCache, err := cache.New(config, opts)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		ic.Cache = baseCache
+		return ic, nil
+	}
+	return ic, fn
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformer(ctx context.Context, obj client.Object, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(fmt.Sprintf("%T", obj))
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformer(ctx, obj, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformerForKind(ctx context.Context, gvk schema.GroupVersionKind, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(gvk.Kind)
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformerForKind(ctx, gvk, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) hasWatch(kind string) bool {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	defer c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.requestedWatches.Has(kind)
+}
+
+type quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec struct {
+	featureGateEnabled bool
+	wantWatched        bool
+}
+
+var _ = ginkgo.Describe("Manager quota automation feature", ginkgo.Label("area:multikueue", "feature:multikueue"), ginkgo.Ordered, ginkgo.ContinueOnFailure, func() {
+	ginkgo.AfterEach(func() {
+		managerTestCluster.fwk.StopManager(managerTestCluster.ctx)
+	})
+
+	ginkgo.DescribeTable("Quota automation feature gate verification", func(spec quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec) {
+		features.SetFeatureGateDuringTest(ginkgo.GinkgoTB(), features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation, spec.featureGateEnabled)
+
+		ic, newCacheFunc := newInterceptedCache()
+		managerTestCluster.fwk.StartManager(managerTestCluster.ctx, managerTestCluster.cfg, func(ctx context.Context, mgr manager.Manager) {
+			err := multikueue.SetupIndexer(ctx, mgr.GetFieldIndexer(), managersConfigNamespace.Name)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			adapters, err := jobframework.GetMultiKueueAdapters(defaultEnabledIntegrations)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			err = multikueue.SetupControllers(mgr, managersConfigNamespace.Name,
+				multikueue.WithGCInterval(2*time.Second),
+				multikueue.WithWorkerLostTimeout(testingWorkerLostTimeout),
+				multikueue.WithEventsBatchPeriod(100*time.Millisecond),
+				multikueue.WithAdapters(adapters),
+				multikueue.WithDispatcherName(config.MultiKueueDispatcherModeAllAtOnce),
+			)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			failedWebhook, err := webhooks.Setup(mgr, nil)
+			gomega.Expect(err).ToNot(gomega.HaveOccurred(), "webhook", failedWebhook)
+		}, framework.WithNewCache(newCacheFunc))
+
+		time.Sleep(3 * time.Second)
```


  When expecting the watch to have happened, I could indeed use `Eventually`.
  
  However, when expecting the watch **not** to have happened, when FG = disabled (which is what I really care about in this test), I don't see a way to employ `Eventually` in the right way. 
  Initially "watch has happened" will be certainly false, and I don't have a good "hook" to wait until.
  
  From what I see, such cases are often handled with `Consistently`, which in this case is actually equivalent to a custom sleep & check then. (Because, once "watch has happened", it will never "un-have-happened").

```diff
+)
+
+type workerState struct {
+	lqs      []*kueue.LocalQueue
+	cqs      []*kueue.ClusterQueue
+	inactive bool
+}
+
+func TestCQReconcile(t *testing.T) {
+	features.SetFeatureGateDuringTest(t, features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation, true)
+
+	cases := map[string]struct {

+
+		wantQuotaAutomated      bool

+		wantAutomationCondition bool

+		wantMessage             string

+				Obj(),

+					Obj(),
+			},
+			configs: []*kueue.MultiKueueConfig{

+			},
+			workers: map[string]workerState{
+				"worker1": {
+					lqs: []*kueue.LocalQueue{utiltestingapi.MakeLocalQueue("lq1", TestNamespace).ClusterQueue("w1-cq1").Obj()},
+					cqs: []*kueue.ClusterQueue{
+						utiltestingapi.MakeClusterQueue("w1-cq1").

+							Obj(),
+					},
+				},
+				"worker2": {

+		},
+		"multiple flavors and incompatible ResourceGroups across workers": {

+
+	for name, tc := range cases {

+				WithObjects(asObjs(tc.configs)...).
+				WithIndex(&kueue.AdmissionCheck{}, AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey, admissionCheckControllerNameIndexerFunc).
+				WithIndex(&kueue.AdmissionCheck{}, AdmissionCheckUsingConfigKey, admissioncheck.IndexerByConfigFunction(kueue.MultiKueueControllerName, configGVK)).
+				WithIndex(&kueue.ClusterQueue{}, ClusterQueueAdmissionChecksKey, clusterQueueAdmissionChecksIndexerFunc).
+				WithStatusSubresource(tc.cq).
+				Build()
+
+			adapters, _ := jobframework.GetMultiKueueAdapters(sets.New("batch/job"))
+			cRec := newClustersReconciler(c, TestNamespace, 0, defaultOrigin, nil, adapters, nil, nil)
+			cRec.rootContext = ctx
+
+			for worker, wState := range tc.workers {
+				workerClient := utiltesting.NewClientBuilder().
+					WithObjects(asObjs(wState.cqs)...).
+					WithObjects(asObjs(wState.lqs)...).
+					Build()
+				rc := newRemoteClient(c, nil, nil, nil, defaultOrigin, worker, adapters)
+				rc.client = workerClient
+				rc.connecting.Store(wState.inactive)
+				cRec.remoteClients[worker] = rc
+			}
+
+			helper, _ := admissioncheck.NewMultiKueueStoreHelper(c)
+			reconciler := newCQReconciler(c, helper, cRec, nil)
+
+			_, gotErr := reconciler.Reconcile(ctx, reconcile.Request{NamespacedName: types.NamespacedName{Name: tc.cq.Name}})
+			if gotErr != nil {
+				t.Errorf("unexpected reconcile error: %v", gotErr)
+			}
+
+			gotCQ := &kueue.ClusterQueue{}
+			if err := c.Get(ctx, types.NamespacedName{Name: tc.cq.Name}, gotCQ); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("failed to fetch local ClusterQueue: %v", err)
+			}
+
+			// Verify quotas
+			if !tc.wantQuotaAutomated {

+				}
+			} else {
+				if len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 {
+					t.Fatalf("expected exactly 1 resource group, got: %d", len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups))
+				}
+				if len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+					t.Fatalf("expected exactly 1 resource flavor, got: %d", len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors))
+				}
+				quotas := gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0].Resources
+
+				if len(quotas) != len(tc.wantNominalQuotas) {
+					t.Errorf("expected exactly %d nominal quotas, got: %d", len(tc.wantNominalQuotas), len(quotas))
+				}
+
+				for _, resQuota := range quotas {
+					expectedVal, exists := tc.wantNominalQuotas[string(resQuota.Name)]
+					if !exists {
+						t.Errorf("unexpected resource quota: %s", resQuota.Name)
+						continue
+					}
+					expectedQty := resource.MustParse(expectedVal)
+					if !resQuota.NominalQuota.Equal(expectedQty) {
+						t.Errorf("expected resource %q nominalQuota: %s, got: %s", resQuota.Name, expectedVal, resQuota.NominalQuota.String())
+					}
+				}
+			}
+
+			// Verify condition state
+			cond := apimeta.FindStatusCondition(gotCQ.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation)
+			if tc.wantAutomationCondition {
+				if cond == nil {
+					t.Error("expected status condition to be defined, got nil")
+				} else {
+					expectedStatus := metav1.ConditionFalse
+					if tc.wantQuotaAutomated {
+						expectedStatus = metav1.ConditionTrue
+					}
+					if cond.Status != expectedStatus {
+						t.Errorf("expected status: %s, got: %s", expectedStatus, cond.Status)
+					}
+					if cond.Reason != tc.wantReason {
+						t.Errorf("expected reason: %s, got: %s", tc.wantReason, cond.Reason)
+					}
+					if cond.Message != tc.wantMessage {
+						t.Errorf("expected message: %q, got: %q", tc.wantMessage, cond.Message)
+					}
+				}
+			} else {
+				if cond != nil {
+					t.Errorf("expected status condition to be absent, got: %v", cond)
+				}
+			}
```

  nit, but I would prefer to use cmp.Diff to make the assert less verbose and more controlled

```diff
+		err := rc.startGenericWorkloadWatcher(watchCtx, kind, watcher)
```

  Something is off here: we call `startGenericWorkloadWatcher` so the name suggests we have "Workloads", but OTOH it seems that "kind" can be any type which is a key in the `rc.adapters` map. So either the function is mis-named, or we call it incorrectly.

### Review by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) (commented) — 2026-05-18T13:48:02Z

- **pkg/controller/admissionchecks/multikueue/multikueuecluster.go:223** by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) — 2026-05-18T13:48:01Z
```diff
@@ -212,26 +220,45 @@ func (rc *remoteClient) setConfig(watchCtx context.Context, config *clientConfig
 		if !implementsWatcher {
 			continue
 		}

+		err := rc.startGenericWorkloadWatcher(watchCtx, kind, watcher)
```

  Please note the name says "**generic** workload". 
  I intended it to be analogous to "**generic** job", i.e. a much broader class than a `Job`.
  
  This function is supposed to be called:
  - for `Workload` 
  - for any generic job kind that is registered in `rc.adapters`.
  
  For that, "generic workload" was the best naming I could come up with.
  Any other ideas?

### Review by [@mimowo](https://github.com/mimowo) (commented) — 2026-05-18T14:03:36Z

- **test/integration/multikueue/setup_test.go:1248** by [@mimowo](https://github.com/mimowo) — 2026-05-18T14:03:36Z
```diff
@@ -1160,3 +1169,93 @@ var _ = ginkgo.Describe("MultiKueue with ClusterProfile", ginkgo.Label("area:mul
 		})
 	})
 })
+
+type interceptedCache struct {
+	cache.Cache
+	mu               sync.Mutex
+	requestedWatches sets.Set[string]
+}
+
+func newInterceptedCache() (*interceptedCache, cache.NewCacheFunc) {
+	ic := &interceptedCache{
+		requestedWatches: sets.New[string](),
+	}
+	fn := func(config *rest.Config, opts cache.Options) (cache.Cache, error) {
+		baseCache, err := cache.New(config, opts)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		ic.Cache = baseCache
+		return ic, nil
+	}
+	return ic, fn
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformer(ctx context.Context, obj client.Object, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(fmt.Sprintf("%T", obj))
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformer(ctx, obj, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformerForKind(ctx context.Context, gvk schema.GroupVersionKind, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(gvk.Kind)
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformerForKind(ctx, gvk, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) hasWatch(kind string) bool {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	defer c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.requestedWatches.Has(kind)
+}
+
+type quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec struct {
+	featureGateEnabled bool
+	wantWatched        bool
+}
+
+var _ = ginkgo.Describe("Manager quota automation feature", ginkgo.Label("area:multikueue", "feature:multikueue"), ginkgo.Ordered, ginkgo.ContinueOnFailure, func() {
+	ginkgo.AfterEach(func() {
+		managerTestCluster.fwk.StopManager(managerTestCluster.ctx)
+	})
+
+	ginkgo.DescribeTable("Quota automation feature gate verification", func(spec quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec) {
+		features.SetFeatureGateDuringTest(ginkgo.GinkgoTB(), features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation, spec.featureGateEnabled)
+
+		ic, newCacheFunc := newInterceptedCache()
+		managerTestCluster.fwk.StartManager(managerTestCluster.ctx, managerTestCluster.cfg, func(ctx context.Context, mgr manager.Manager) {
+			err := multikueue.SetupIndexer(ctx, mgr.GetFieldIndexer(), managersConfigNamespace.Name)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			adapters, err := jobframework.GetMultiKueueAdapters(defaultEnabledIntegrations)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			err = multikueue.SetupControllers(mgr, managersConfigNamespace.Name,
+				multikueue.WithGCInterval(2*time.Second),
+				multikueue.WithWorkerLostTimeout(testingWorkerLostTimeout),
+				multikueue.WithEventsBatchPeriod(100*time.Millisecond),
+				multikueue.WithAdapters(adapters),
+				multikueue.WithDispatcherName(config.MultiKueueDispatcherModeAllAtOnce),
+			)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			failedWebhook, err := webhooks.Setup(mgr, nil)
+			gomega.Expect(err).ToNot(gomega.HaveOccurred(), "webhook", failedWebhook)
+		}, framework.WithNewCache(newCacheFunc))
+
+		time.Sleep(3 * time.Second)
```

  I see, in that case I would suggest to actually stop using DescribeTable.
  
  When we have two separate tests we can easily use Eventuaully in one, and Consistently in the other without any "if's". 
  
  I know this would be just a single "if" now, so maybe let's keep "DescribeTable" but my practice so far shows that the "DescribeTable" are notoriously hard to evolve when the paths diverge as it results in more and more "ifs".

```diff
+func (rc *remoteClient) startQueueWatchers(ctx context.Context) error {

+		}

+			rc.queueEventsForLQ(ctx, lq)

+func (rc *remoteClient) queueEventsForCQ(ctx context.Context, remoteCQ *kueue.ClusterQueue) {
+	log := ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).WithValues("remoteCQ", remoteCQ.Name)
+
+	// Remote clients aren't indexed, so we fetch all remote LQs.
+	remoteLQs := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+	if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteLQs); err != nil {
```

  I think not easily.
  
  The problem: `rc` is a remote client, with:
  - no caching (and hence no indexing)
  - no support for API-side filtering by `.Spec.ClusterQueue` because it's not listed among `selectableFields` in the LocalQueue CRD.
  
  My preferred way forward would be to make this remote client cached, so that it can be indexed.
  However, I'd rather *not* make it cached for all types (given that Workloads volume can be high).
  I'd rather make it selective per kind - e.g. for LQs and CQs, but not Workloads. 
  This is exactly [this future work idea](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/tree/main/keps/9988-multikueue-manager-quota-automation#use-cached-remote-clients-for-low-volume-resource-kinds) from the KEP.
  
  So - my proposal is to:
  
  - leave it as is for Alpha, where those "maybe heavy" `List`s are hidden behind FG anyway
  - plan for kind-selective caching for Beta
  
  WDYT?

### Review by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) (commented) — 2026-05-18T14:21:26Z

- **apis/kueue/v1beta2/clusterqueue_types.go:32** by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) — 2026-05-18T14:21:26Z
```diff
@@ -22,6 +22,16 @@ import (
 	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
 )
 
+// ClusterQueue status condition types.

+	// ClusterQueueActive indicates that the ClusterQueue can admit new workloads and its quota

```

  I think I understand the point.
  
  However, note that your proposal is also, strictly syntactically, a noun phrase :) ("quota" being root of it).
  
  To make it truly "adjectival", we'd need to say `HavingX`, `AutomatedByX`, `WithXEnabled`... but in my eyes it'd make the whole name just too long and harder to parse. While this nominal `...Automation` feels to me easy to grasp: of course it's about "being affected by that thing".
  
  WDYT?


  I think it would be nice to have some kind of alias for string in the context of MultiKueue jobs, like `MultiKueueJob`, similarly as we have ClusterQueueReference etc. Then we could have two functions like `startWorkloadWatcher` and `startMultiKueueJobWatcher` which use strong typing. However, this is largely a pre-existing concern for what I can see, so it is not a blocker to me. Still, some good ideas for long term are welcome ofc.

  In my eyes, the main value of DescribeTable here is that it verifies that the test for the "disabled" case actually tests the right thing.
  
  That's because the crucial line in this test looks like this:
  ```golang
  gomega.Expect(ic.hasWatch("*v1beta2.ClusterQueue")).To(...)
  ```
  
  And now, how do we know that `*v1beta2.ClusterQueue` is the right value to be there?
  How do we ensure that this test remains meaningful e.g. when `ClusterQueue` gets migrated to `v1beta3`?
  The best way to know is to run this test in the "enabled" case, and verify that the result is different.
  
  But now, if I "fork" this into separate "enabled" & "disabled" stories, I expect the "enabled" story to evolve like this: "here, let's check the user-observable outcome, and don't care about this watch existence, why would we care about that".
  
  And that way, we'll leave the "disabled" story without a nice self-verification.
  
  I'm still thinking how to incorporate your feedback without losing this bit.

  I also have a hunch that this might be heavy, because IIUC this call will be made for every change in any CQ on any worker. I think those events won't be deduped, only the events in the workqueue (`cqUpdateCh`) will be deduped, which is after the List is called. So this List call would be made constantly.

```diff
@@ -1160,3 +1169,93 @@ var _ = ginkgo.Describe("MultiKueue with ClusterProfile", ginkgo.Label("area:mul
 		})
 	})
 })
+
+type interceptedCache struct {
+	cache.Cache
+	mu               sync.Mutex
+	requestedWatches sets.Set[string]
+}
+
+func newInterceptedCache() (*interceptedCache, cache.NewCacheFunc) {
+	ic := &interceptedCache{
+		requestedWatches: sets.New[string](),
+	}
+	fn := func(config *rest.Config, opts cache.Options) (cache.Cache, error) {
+		baseCache, err := cache.New(config, opts)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		ic.Cache = baseCache
+		return ic, nil
+	}
+	return ic, fn
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformer(ctx context.Context, obj client.Object, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(fmt.Sprintf("%T", obj))
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformer(ctx, obj, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformerForKind(ctx context.Context, gvk schema.GroupVersionKind, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(gvk.Kind)
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformerForKind(ctx, gvk, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) hasWatch(kind string) bool {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	defer c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.requestedWatches.Has(kind)
+}
+
+type quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec struct {
+	featureGateEnabled bool
+	wantWatched        bool
+}
+
+var _ = ginkgo.Describe("Manager quota automation feature", ginkgo.Label("area:multikueue", "feature:multikueue"), ginkgo.Ordered, ginkgo.ContinueOnFailure, func() {

+
+		ic, newCacheFunc := newInterceptedCache()

+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			adapters, err := jobframework.GetMultiKueueAdapters(defaultEnabledIntegrations)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			err = multikueue.SetupControllers(mgr, managersConfigNamespace.Name,
+				multikueue.WithGCInterval(2*time.Second),
+				multikueue.WithWorkerLostTimeout(testingWorkerLostTimeout),
+				multikueue.WithEventsBatchPeriod(100*time.Millisecond),
+				multikueue.WithAdapters(adapters),
+				multikueue.WithDispatcherName(config.MultiKueueDispatcherModeAllAtOnce),
+			)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			failedWebhook, err := webhooks.Setup(mgr, nil)
+			gomega.Expect(err).ToNot(gomega.HaveOccurred(), "webhook", failedWebhook)
+		}, framework.WithNewCache(newCacheFunc))
+
+		time.Sleep(3 * time.Second)
```


  > the problem with constant heavy Lists
  
  Just to set the stage: I think "**constant**" is the problem (while "heavy" not so much).
  
  @kshalot just pointed out that the current code is going to run the whole reconciling routine on every change of every remote CQ (even its status!), which is clearly sub-optimal.
  
  Instead, we should make sure work is triggered only on a remote CQ quota change. (Or creation, or deletion). I think that, once we ensure that, even "heavy" `List` will become acceptable for Alpha.
  
  So I think Alpha needs some caching. Maybe with Informers.
  (And then, once we have this, heavy Lists won't be a heavy problem, and may be solvable with that as well).

```diff
+func TestCQReconcile(t *testing.T) {
+	features.SetFeatureGateDuringTest(t, features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation, true)
+
+	cases := map[string]struct {
+		cq      *kueue.ClusterQueue
+		lqs     []*kueue.LocalQueue
+		acs     []*kueue.AdmissionCheck
+		configs []*kueue.MultiKueueConfig
+		workers map[string]workerState
+
+		wantQuotaAutomated      bool
+		wantNominalQuotas       map[string]string // Ignored if wantQuotaAutomated == false
+		wantAutomationCondition bool
+		wantReason              string
+		wantMessage             string
+	}{
+		"multiple resources for single LQs and CQs": {
+			cq: utiltestingapi.MakeClusterQueue("cq1").
+				ResourceGroup(*utiltestingapi.MakeFlavorQuotas("default").Resource("cpu", "0").Resource("memory", "0").Obj()).
+				AdmissionChecks("ac1").
+				Obj(),
+			lqs: []*kueue.LocalQueue{
+				utiltestingapi.MakeLocalQueue("lq1", TestNamespace).ClusterQueue("cq1").Obj(),
+			},
+			acs: []*kueue.AdmissionCheck{
+				utiltestingapi.MakeAdmissionCheck("ac1").
+					ControllerName(kueue.MultiKueueControllerName).
+					Parameters(kueue.GroupVersion.Group, "MultiKueueConfig", "config1").
+					Obj(),

+
+	for name, tc := range cases {
+		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			ctx, _ := utiltesting.ContextWithLog(t)
+			c := utiltesting.NewClientBuilder().
+				WithObjects(tc.cq).
+				WithObjects(asObjs(tc.lqs)...).
+				WithObjects(asObjs(tc.acs)...).
+				WithObjects(asObjs(tc.configs)...).
+				WithIndex(&kueue.AdmissionCheck{}, AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey, admissionCheckControllerNameIndexerFunc).
+				WithIndex(&kueue.AdmissionCheck{}, AdmissionCheckUsingConfigKey, admissioncheck.IndexerByConfigFunction(kueue.MultiKueueControllerName, configGVK)).
+				WithIndex(&kueue.ClusterQueue{}, ClusterQueueAdmissionChecksKey, clusterQueueAdmissionChecksIndexerFunc).
+				WithStatusSubresource(tc.cq).
+				Build()
+
+			adapters, _ := jobframework.GetMultiKueueAdapters(sets.New("batch/job"))
+			cRec := newClustersReconciler(c, TestNamespace, 0, defaultOrigin, nil, adapters, nil, nil)
+			cRec.rootContext = ctx
+
+			for worker, wState := range tc.workers {
+				workerClient := utiltesting.NewClientBuilder().
+					WithObjects(asObjs(wState.cqs)...).
+					WithObjects(asObjs(wState.lqs)...).
+					Build()
+				rc := newRemoteClient(c, nil, nil, nil, defaultOrigin, worker, adapters)
+				rc.client = workerClient
+				rc.connecting.Store(wState.inactive)
+				cRec.remoteClients[worker] = rc
+			}
+
+			helper, _ := admissioncheck.NewMultiKueueStoreHelper(c)
+			reconciler := newCQReconciler(c, helper, cRec, nil)
+
+			_, gotErr := reconciler.Reconcile(ctx, reconcile.Request{NamespacedName: types.NamespacedName{Name: tc.cq.Name}})
+			if gotErr != nil {
+				t.Errorf("unexpected reconcile error: %v", gotErr)
+			}
+
+			gotCQ := &kueue.ClusterQueue{}
+			if err := c.Get(ctx, types.NamespacedName{Name: tc.cq.Name}, gotCQ); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("failed to fetch local ClusterQueue: %v", err)
+			}
+
+			// Verify quotas
+			if !tc.wantQuotaAutomated {
+				if diff := cmp.Diff(tc.cq.Spec.ResourceGroups, gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups); diff != "" {
+					t.Errorf("unexpected resourceGroups changes for quota automation disabled (-want/+got):\n%s", diff)
+				}
+			} else {
+				if len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 {
+					t.Fatalf("expected exactly 1 resource group, got: %d", len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups))
+				}
+				if len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+					t.Fatalf("expected exactly 1 resource flavor, got: %d", len(gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors))
+				}
+				quotas := gotCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0].Resources
+
+				if len(quotas) != len(tc.wantNominalQuotas) {
+					t.Errorf("expected exactly %d nominal quotas, got: %d", len(tc.wantNominalQuotas), len(quotas))
+				}
+
+				for _, resQuota := range quotas {
+					expectedVal, exists := tc.wantNominalQuotas[string(resQuota.Name)]
+					if !exists {
+						t.Errorf("unexpected resource quota: %s", resQuota.Name)
+						continue
+					}
+					expectedQty := resource.MustParse(expectedVal)
+					if !resQuota.NominalQuota.Equal(expectedQty) {
+						t.Errorf("expected resource %q nominalQuota: %s, got: %s", resQuota.Name, expectedVal, resQuota.NominalQuota.String())
+					}
+				}
+			}
+
+			// Verify condition state
+			cond := apimeta.FindStatusCondition(gotCQ.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation)
+			if tc.wantAutomationCondition {
+				if cond == nil {
+					t.Error("expected status condition to be defined, got nil")
+				} else {
+					expectedStatus := metav1.ConditionFalse
+					if tc.wantQuotaAutomated {
+						expectedStatus = metav1.ConditionTrue
+					}
+					if cond.Status != expectedStatus {
+						t.Errorf("expected status: %s, got: %s", expectedStatus, cond.Status)
+					}
+					if cond.Reason != tc.wantReason {
+						t.Errorf("expected reason: %s, got: %s", tc.wantReason, cond.Reason)
+					}
+					if cond.Message != tc.wantMessage {
+						t.Errorf("expected message: %q, got: %q", tc.wantMessage, cond.Message)
+					}
+				}
+			} else {
+				if cond != nil {
+					t.Errorf("expected status condition to be absent, got: %v", cond)
+				}
+			}
```

```diff
+		err := rc.startGenericWorkloadWatcher(watchCtx, kind, watcher)
```

  I mean that the `kind` is a string parameter which makes it possible to pass to `startWorkloadOrJobWatcher` virtually anything (while only Workload, or Job kind names make sense), but interestingly it is not used for anything except just logging.

### Review by [@olekzabl](https://github.com/olekzabl) (commented) — 2026-05-19T10:58:19Z

```diff
@@ -1160,3 +1169,93 @@ var _ = ginkgo.Describe("MultiKueue with ClusterProfile", ginkgo.Label("area:mul
 		})
 	})
 })
+
+type interceptedCache struct {
+	cache.Cache
+	mu               sync.Mutex
+	requestedWatches sets.Set[string]
+}
+
+func newInterceptedCache() (*interceptedCache, cache.NewCacheFunc) {
+	ic := &interceptedCache{
+		requestedWatches: sets.New[string](),
+	}
+	fn := func(config *rest.Config, opts cache.Options) (cache.Cache, error) {
+		baseCache, err := cache.New(config, opts)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		ic.Cache = baseCache
+		return ic, nil
+	}
+	return ic, fn
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformer(ctx context.Context, obj client.Object, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(fmt.Sprintf("%T", obj))
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformer(ctx, obj, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) GetInformerForKind(ctx context.Context, gvk schema.GroupVersionKind, opts ...cache.InformerGetOption) (cache.Informer, error) {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	c.requestedWatches.Insert(gvk.Kind)
+	c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.Cache.GetInformerForKind(ctx, gvk, opts...)
+}
+
+func (c *interceptedCache) hasWatch(kind string) bool {
+	c.mu.Lock()
+	defer c.mu.Unlock()
+	return c.requestedWatches.Has(kind)
+}
+
+type quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec struct {
+	featureGateEnabled bool
+	wantWatched        bool
+}
+

+	ginkgo.DescribeTable("Quota automation feature gate verification", func(spec quotaAutomationFeatureGateTestSpec) {
+		features.SetFeatureGateDuringTest(ginkgo.GinkgoTB(), features.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation, spec.featureGateEnabled)
+
+		ic, newCacheFunc := newInterceptedCache()
+		managerTestCluster.fwk.StartManager(managerTestCluster.ctx, managerTestCluster.cfg, func(ctx context.Context, mgr manager.Manager) {
+			err := multikueue.SetupIndexer(ctx, mgr.GetFieldIndexer(), managersConfigNamespace.Name)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			adapters, err := jobframework.GetMultiKueueAdapters(defaultEnabledIntegrations)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			err = multikueue.SetupControllers(mgr, managersConfigNamespace.Name,
+				multikueue.WithGCInterval(2*time.Second),
+				multikueue.WithWorkerLostTimeout(testingWorkerLostTimeout),
+				multikueue.WithEventsBatchPeriod(100*time.Millisecond),
+				multikueue.WithAdapters(adapters),
+				multikueue.WithDispatcherName(config.MultiKueueDispatcherModeAllAtOnce),
+			)
+			gomega.Expect(err).NotTo(gomega.HaveOccurred())
+
+			failedWebhook, err := webhooks.Setup(mgr, nil)
+			gomega.Expect(err).ToNot(gomega.HaveOccurred(), "webhook", failedWebhook)
+		}, framework.WithNewCache(newCacheFunc))
+
+		time.Sleep(3 * time.Second)

```

  I added other meaningful test cases higher up in this file.
  The new cases check reaction to remote LQ / CQ changes, specifically they check the manager-side quotas.
  As for conditions - I felt no much value in checking those; this is already covered extensively by unit tests.
  
  I chose to keep those "user-observable effects" tests separately from the "watcher exists or not" tests, due to diverging test setup. ("User-observable effects" could be easily unified with the main Describe() block; "watcher exists or not" feels quite different).

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,450 @@
+/*

+limitations under the License.

+package multikueue
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"fmt"
+
+	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/equality"
+	apierrors "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
+	apimeta "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/meta"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
+	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/types"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/sets"
+	"k8s.io/client-go/util/workqueue"
+	"k8s.io/utils/ptr"
+	ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/builder"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/controller"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/event"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/handler"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/reconcile"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/source"
+
+	kueue "sigs.k8s.io/kueue/apis/kueue/v1beta2"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/controller/core/indexer"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/util/admissioncheck"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/kueue/pkg/util/roletracker"
+)
+
+type CQReconciler struct {
+	client      client.Client
+	helper      *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper
+	clusters    *clustersReconciler
+	roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker
+}
+
+var _ reconcile.Reconciler = (*CQReconciler)(nil)
+
+func newCQReconciler(c client.Client, helper *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper, clusters *clustersReconciler, roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker) *CQReconciler {
+	return &CQReconciler{
+		client:      c,
+		helper:      helper,
+		clusters:    clusters,
+		roleTracker: roleTracker,
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req reconcile.Request) (reconcile.Result, error) {
+	log := ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).WithValues("clusterQueue", req.Name)
+	log.V(3).Info("Reconcile ClusterQueue event received (in the MultiKueue controller)")
+
+	cq := &kueue.ClusterQueue{}
+	if err := r.client.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, cq); err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
+	}
+
+	ac, hasAC, err := r.getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx, cq)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	if !hasAC {
+		log.V(3).Info("Not a MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue, skipping reconcile.")
+		err := r.removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	log.V(2).Info("Reconciling MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue")
+
+	cfg, err := r.helper.ConfigFromRef(ctx, ac.Spec.Parameters)
+	if err != nil {
+		if apierrors.IsNotFound(err) {
+			err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "The referenced MultiKueueConfig was not found.")
+		}
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if ptr.Deref(cfg.Spec.QuotaManagement, kueue.QuotaManagementManual) == kueue.QuotaManagementManual {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "NotRequested", "MultiKueue manager quota automation has not been requested.")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 || len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(
+			ctx,
+			cq,
+			metav1.ConditionFalse,
+			"UnsupportedConfiguration",
+			"Quota automation requires that the manager-side ClusterQueue has exactly one ResourceFlavor",
+		)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	singleFlavor := &cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0]
+
+	aggregatedQuotas, err := r.aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx, cq, cfg)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	covered := sets.New(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources...)
+	remoteResources := sets.KeySet(aggregatedQuotas)
+	missingResources := remoteResources.Difference(covered)
+	if missingResources.Len() > 0 {
+		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("manager-side coveredResources is missing resources configured on workers: %v", sets.List(missingResources))
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", errMsg)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	var newResources []kueue.ResourceQuota
+	for _, resName := range cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources {
+		newResources = append(newResources, kueue.ResourceQuota{
+			Name:         resName,
+			NominalQuota: aggregatedQuotas[resName],
+		})
+	}
+
+	if !equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(singleFlavor.Resources, newResources) {
+		singleFlavor.Resources = newResources
+		if err := r.client.Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+			return reconcile.Result{}, fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue nominal quotas: %w", err)
+		}
+	}
+
+	err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionTrue, "QuotaAutomated", "ClusterQueue quota is automatically managed based on MultiKueue workers.")
+	return reconcile.Result{}, err
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) (*kueue.AdmissionCheck, bool, error) {
+	if cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy == nil {
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+
+	acList := &kueue.AdmissionCheckList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey: kueue.MultiKueueControllerName}); err != nil {
+		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("listing local AdmissionChecks: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	cqACNames := sets.New[string]()
+	for _, rule := range cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy.AdmissionChecks {
+		cqACNames.Insert(string(rule.Name))
+	}
+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {
+		if cqACNames.Has(ac.Name) {
+			return &ac, true, nil
+		}
+	}
+
+	return nil, false, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, cfg *kueue.MultiKueueConfig) (map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity, error) {
+	localLQs := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, localLQs, client.MatchingFields{indexer.QueueClusterQueueKey: cq.Name}); err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("listing local LocalQueues: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	lqKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+	for _, llq := range localLQs.Items {
+		lqKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: llq.Namespace, Name: llq.Name})
+	}
+
+	total := make(map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity)
+
+	for _, workerName := range cfg.Spec.Clusters {
+		rc, found := r.clusters.controllerFor(workerName)
+		if !found {
+			ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).V(3).Info("Worker cluster client not found, skipping it in quota aggregation", "workerCluster", workerName)
+			continue
+		}
+		if rc.connecting.Load() {
+			ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).V(3).Info("Worker cluster client still connecting, skipping it in quota aggregation", "workerCluster", workerName)
+			continue
+		}
+		remoteLQList := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+		// This List is cached (by the selectivelyCachingClient).
+		if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteLQList); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		remoteCQKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+		for _, rlq := range remoteLQList.Items {
+			if lqKeys.Has(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: rlq.Namespace, Name: rlq.Name}) {
+				remoteCQKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Name: string(rlq.Spec.ClusterQueue)})
+			}
+		}
+
+		remoteCQList := &kueue.ClusterQueueList{}
+		// This List is cached (by the selectivelyCachingClient).
+		if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteCQList); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+
+		for _, rcq := range remoteCQList.Items {
+			key := types.NamespacedName{Name: rcq.Name}
+			if !remoteCQKeys.Has(key) {
+				continue
+			}
+			for _, rg := range rcq.Spec.ResourceGroups {
+				for _, flavor := range rg.Flavors {
+					for _, res := range flavor.Resources {
+						curr := total[res.Name]
+						curr.Add(res.NominalQuota)
+						total[res.Name] = curr
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return total, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) error {
+	if !apimeta.RemoveStatusCondition(&cq.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if err := r.client.Status().Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("removing ClusterQueue condition status: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, status metav1.ConditionStatus, reason, message string) error {
+	newCondition := metav1.Condition{
+		Type:               kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation,
+		Status:             status,
+		Reason:             reason,
+		Message:            message,
+		ObservedGeneration: cq.Generation,
+	}
+
+	oldCondition := apimeta.FindStatusCondition(cq.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation)
+	if cmpConditionState(oldCondition, &newCondition) {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	apimeta.SetStatusCondition(&cq.Status.Conditions, newCondition)
+	if err := r.client.Status().Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue condition status: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) queueEventsForAC(ctx context.Context, acName string, q workqueue.TypedRateLimitingInterface[reconcile.Request]) {
```

  We already have the function `ClusterQueuesUsingAdmissionCheck`, any reason not to use it?

```diff
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req reconcile.Request) (reconcile.Result, error) {
+	log := ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).WithValues("clusterQueue", req.Name)
+	log.V(3).Info("Reconcile ClusterQueue event received (in the MultiKueue controller)")

+
+	cfg, err := r.helper.ConfigFromRef(ctx, ac.Spec.Parameters)
+	if err != nil {
+		if apierrors.IsNotFound(err) {
+			err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "The referenced MultiKueueConfig was not found.")
+		}
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if ptr.Deref(cfg.Spec.QuotaManagement, kueue.QuotaManagementManual) == kueue.QuotaManagementManual {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "NotRequested", "MultiKueue manager quota automation has not been requested.")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 || len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(
+			ctx,
+			cq,
+			metav1.ConditionFalse,
+			"UnsupportedConfiguration",
+			"Quota automation requires that the manager-side ClusterQueue has exactly one ResourceFlavor",
+		)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	singleFlavor := &cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0]
+
+	aggregatedQuotas, err := r.aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx, cq, cfg)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	covered := sets.New(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources...)
+	remoteResources := sets.KeySet(aggregatedQuotas)
+	missingResources := remoteResources.Difference(covered)
+	if missingResources.Len() > 0 {
+		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("manager-side coveredResources is missing resources configured on workers: %v", sets.List(missingResources))
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", errMsg)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	var newResources []kueue.ResourceQuota
+	for _, resName := range cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources {
+		newResources = append(newResources, kueue.ResourceQuota{
+			Name:         resName,
+			NominalQuota: aggregatedQuotas[resName],
+		})
+	}
+
+	if !equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(singleFlavor.Resources, newResources) {
+		singleFlavor.Resources = newResources
+		if err := r.client.Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+			return reconcile.Result{}, fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue nominal quotas: %w", err)
+		}
+	}
+
+	err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionTrue, "QuotaAutomated", "ClusterQueue quota is automatically managed based on MultiKueue workers.")
+	return reconcile.Result{}, err
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) (*kueue.AdmissionCheck, bool, error) {
+	if cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy == nil {
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+
+	acList := &kueue.AdmissionCheckList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey: kueue.MultiKueueControllerName}); err != nil {
+		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("listing local AdmissionChecks: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	cqACNames := sets.New[string]()
+	for _, rule := range cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy.AdmissionChecks {
+		cqACNames.Insert(string(rule.Name))
+	}
+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {
+		if cqACNames.Has(ac.Name) {
+			return &ac, true, nil
+		}
+	}
+
+	return nil, false, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, cfg *kueue.MultiKueueConfig) (map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity, error) {
+	localLQs := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, localLQs, client.MatchingFields{indexer.QueueClusterQueueKey: cq.Name}); err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("listing local LocalQueues: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	lqKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+	for _, llq := range localLQs.Items {
+		lqKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: llq.Namespace, Name: llq.Name})
+	}
+
+	total := make(map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity)
+
+	for _, workerName := range cfg.Spec.Clusters {
+		rc, found := r.clusters.controllerFor(workerName)
+		if !found {
+			ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).V(3).Info("Worker cluster client not found, skipping it in quota aggregation", "workerCluster", workerName)
+			continue
+		}
+		if rc.connecting.Load() {
+			ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).V(3).Info("Worker cluster client still connecting, skipping it in quota aggregation", "workerCluster", workerName)
+			continue
+		}
+		remoteLQList := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+		// This List is cached (by the selectivelyCachingClient).
+		if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteLQList); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		remoteCQKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+		for _, rlq := range remoteLQList.Items {
+			if lqKeys.Has(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: rlq.Namespace, Name: rlq.Name}) {
+				remoteCQKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Name: string(rlq.Spec.ClusterQueue)})
+			}
+		}
+
+		remoteCQList := &kueue.ClusterQueueList{}
+		// This List is cached (by the selectivelyCachingClient).
+		if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteCQList); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+
+		for _, rcq := range remoteCQList.Items {
+			key := types.NamespacedName{Name: rcq.Name}
+			if !remoteCQKeys.Has(key) {
+				continue
+			}
+			for _, rg := range rcq.Spec.ResourceGroups {
+				for _, flavor := range rg.Flavors {
+					for _, res := range flavor.Resources {
+						curr := total[res.Name]
+						curr.Add(res.NominalQuota)
+						total[res.Name] = curr
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return total, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) error {
+	if !apimeta.RemoveStatusCondition(&cq.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if err := r.client.Status().Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("removing ClusterQueue condition status: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, status metav1.ConditionStatus, reason, message string) error {
+	newCondition := metav1.Condition{
+		Type:               kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation,
+		Status:             status,
+		Reason:             reason,
+		Message:            message,
+		ObservedGeneration: cq.Generation,
+	}
+
+	oldCondition := apimeta.FindStatusCondition(cq.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation)
+	if cmpConditionState(oldCondition, &newCondition) {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	apimeta.SetStatusCondition(&cq.Status.Conditions, newCondition)
+	if err := r.client.Status().Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue condition status: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) queueEventsForAC(ctx context.Context, acName string, q workqueue.TypedRateLimitingInterface[reconcile.Request]) {
+	cqList := &kueue.ClusterQueueList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, cqList, client.MatchingFields{ClusterQueueAdmissionChecksKey: acName}); err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, cq := range cqList.Items {
+		q.Add(reconcile.Request{NamespacedName: types.NamespacedName{Name: cq.Name}})
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) queueEventsForMKConfig(ctx context.Context, configName string, q workqueue.TypedRateLimitingInterface[reconcile.Request]) {
+	acList := &kueue.AdmissionCheckList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckUsingConfigKey: configName}); err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {
+		r.queueEventsForAC(ctx, ac.Name, q)
+	}
+}
+

+	configList := &kueue.MultiKueueConfigList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, configList, client.MatchingFields{UsingMultiKueueClusters: clusterName}); err != nil {
```

```diff
+var _ reconcile.Reconciler = (*CQReconciler)(nil)
+
+func newCQReconciler(c client.Client, helper *admissioncheck.MultiKueueStoreHelper, clusters *clustersReconciler, roleTracker *roletracker.RoleTracker) *CQReconciler {
+	return &CQReconciler{
+		client:      c,
+		helper:      helper,
+		clusters:    clusters,
+		roleTracker: roleTracker,
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req reconcile.Request) (reconcile.Result, error) {
+	log := ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).WithValues("clusterQueue", req.Name)
+	log.V(3).Info("Reconcile ClusterQueue event received (in the MultiKueue controller)")
+
+	cq := &kueue.ClusterQueue{}
+	if err := r.client.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, cq); err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
+	}
+
+	ac, hasAC, err := r.getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx, cq)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	if !hasAC {
+		log.V(3).Info("Not a MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue, skipping reconcile.")
+		err := r.removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	log.V(2).Info("Reconciling MultiKueue manager ClusterQueue")
+
+	cfg, err := r.helper.ConfigFromRef(ctx, ac.Spec.Parameters)
+	if err != nil {
+		if apierrors.IsNotFound(err) {
+			err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", "The referenced MultiKueueConfig was not found.")
+		}
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if ptr.Deref(cfg.Spec.QuotaManagement, kueue.QuotaManagementManual) == kueue.QuotaManagementManual {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "NotRequested", "MultiKueue manager quota automation has not been requested.")
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	if len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups) != 1 || len(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors) != 1 {
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(
+			ctx,
+			cq,
+			metav1.ConditionFalse,
+			"UnsupportedConfiguration",
+			"Quota automation requires that the manager-side ClusterQueue has exactly one ResourceFlavor",
+		)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+	singleFlavor := &cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].Flavors[0]
+
+	aggregatedQuotas, err := r.aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx, cq, cfg)
+	if err != nil {
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	covered := sets.New(cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources...)
+	remoteResources := sets.KeySet(aggregatedQuotas)
+	missingResources := remoteResources.Difference(covered)
+	if missingResources.Len() > 0 {
+		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("manager-side coveredResources is missing resources configured on workers: %v", sets.List(missingResources))
+		err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionFalse, "UnsupportedConfiguration", errMsg)
+		return reconcile.Result{}, err
+	}
+
+	var newResources []kueue.ResourceQuota
+	for _, resName := range cq.Spec.ResourceGroups[0].CoveredResources {
+		newResources = append(newResources, kueue.ResourceQuota{
+			Name:         resName,
+			NominalQuota: aggregatedQuotas[resName],
+		})
+	}
+
+	if !equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(singleFlavor.Resources, newResources) {
+		singleFlavor.Resources = newResources
+		if err := r.client.Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+			return reconcile.Result{}, fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue nominal quotas: %w", err)
+		}
+	}
+
+	err = r.updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx, cq, metav1.ConditionTrue, "QuotaAutomated", "ClusterQueue quota is automatically managed based on MultiKueue workers.")
+	return reconcile.Result{}, err
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) getMultiKueueAdmissionCheck(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) (*kueue.AdmissionCheck, bool, error) {
+	if cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy == nil {
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+
+	acList := &kueue.AdmissionCheckList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckControllerNameKey: kueue.MultiKueueControllerName}); err != nil {
+		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("listing local AdmissionChecks: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	cqACNames := sets.New[string]()
+	for _, rule := range cq.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy.AdmissionChecks {
+		cqACNames.Insert(string(rule.Name))
+	}
+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {
+		if cqACNames.Has(ac.Name) {
+			return &ac, true, nil
+		}
+	}
+
+	return nil, false, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) aggregateWorkerQuotas(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, cfg *kueue.MultiKueueConfig) (map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity, error) {
+	localLQs := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, localLQs, client.MatchingFields{indexer.QueueClusterQueueKey: cq.Name}); err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("listing local LocalQueues: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	lqKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+	for _, llq := range localLQs.Items {
+		lqKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: llq.Namespace, Name: llq.Name})
+	}
+
+	total := make(map[corev1.ResourceName]resource.Quantity)
+
+	for _, workerName := range cfg.Spec.Clusters {
+		rc, found := r.clusters.controllerFor(workerName)
+		if !found {
+			ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).V(3).Info("Worker cluster client not found, skipping it in quota aggregation", "workerCluster", workerName)
+			continue
+		}
+		if rc.connecting.Load() {
+			ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx).V(3).Info("Worker cluster client still connecting, skipping it in quota aggregation", "workerCluster", workerName)
+			continue
+		}
+		remoteLQList := &kueue.LocalQueueList{}
+		// This List is cached (by the selectivelyCachingClient).
+		if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteLQList); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		remoteCQKeys := sets.New[types.NamespacedName]()
+		for _, rlq := range remoteLQList.Items {
+			if lqKeys.Has(types.NamespacedName{Namespace: rlq.Namespace, Name: rlq.Name}) {
+				remoteCQKeys.Insert(types.NamespacedName{Name: string(rlq.Spec.ClusterQueue)})
+			}
+		}
+
+		remoteCQList := &kueue.ClusterQueueList{}
+		// This List is cached (by the selectivelyCachingClient).
+		if err := rc.client.List(ctx, remoteCQList); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+
+		for _, rcq := range remoteCQList.Items {
+			key := types.NamespacedName{Name: rcq.Name}
+			if !remoteCQKeys.Has(key) {
+				continue
+			}
+			for _, rg := range rcq.Spec.ResourceGroups {
+				for _, flavor := range rg.Flavors {
+					for _, res := range flavor.Resources {
+						curr := total[res.Name]
+						curr.Add(res.NominalQuota)
+						total[res.Name] = curr
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return total, nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) removeQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue) error {
+	if !apimeta.RemoveStatusCondition(&cq.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if err := r.client.Status().Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("removing ClusterQueue condition status: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) updateQuotaAutomationCondition(ctx context.Context, cq *kueue.ClusterQueue, status metav1.ConditionStatus, reason, message string) error {
+	newCondition := metav1.Condition{
+		Type:               kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation,
+		Status:             status,
+		Reason:             reason,
+		Message:            message,
+		ObservedGeneration: cq.Generation,
+	}
+
+	oldCondition := apimeta.FindStatusCondition(cq.Status.Conditions, kueue.MultiKueueManagerQuotaAutomation)
+	if cmpConditionState(oldCondition, &newCondition) {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	apimeta.SetStatusCondition(&cq.Status.Conditions, newCondition)
+	if err := r.client.Status().Update(ctx, cq); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("updating ClusterQueue condition status: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) queueEventsForAC(ctx context.Context, acName string, q workqueue.TypedRateLimitingInterface[reconcile.Request]) {
+	cqList := &kueue.ClusterQueueList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, cqList, client.MatchingFields{ClusterQueueAdmissionChecksKey: acName}); err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, cq := range cqList.Items {
+		q.Add(reconcile.Request{NamespacedName: types.NamespacedName{Name: cq.Name}})
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) queueEventsForMKConfig(ctx context.Context, configName string, q workqueue.TypedRateLimitingInterface[reconcile.Request]) {
+	acList := &kueue.AdmissionCheckList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, acList, client.MatchingFields{AdmissionCheckUsingConfigKey: configName}); err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, ac := range acList.Items {
+		r.queueEventsForAC(ctx, ac.Name, q)
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) queueEventsForMKCluster(ctx context.Context, clusterName string, q workqueue.TypedRateLimitingInterface[reconcile.Request]) {
+	configList := &kueue.MultiKueueConfigList{}
+	if err := r.client.List(ctx, configList, client.MatchingFields{UsingMultiKueueClusters: clusterName}); err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+
+	for _, cfg := range configList.Items {
+		r.queueEventsForMKConfig(ctx, cfg.Name, q)
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *CQReconciler) setupWithManager(mgr ctrl.Manager) error {
+	cqEventFilter := predicate.Funcs{
+		UpdateFunc: func(e event.UpdateEvent) bool {
+			oldCQ, okOld := e.ObjectOld.(*kueue.ClusterQueue)
+			newCQ, okNew := e.ObjectNew.(*kueue.ClusterQueue)
+			if !okOld || !okNew {
+				return true
+			}
+
+			if equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(oldCQ.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy, newCQ.Spec.AdmissionChecksStrategy) &&
+				equality.Semantic.DeepEqual(oldCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups, newCQ.Spec.ResourceGroups) {
+				return false
+			}
+			return true
```

```diff
+func (r *CQReconciler) setupWithManager(mgr ctrl.Manager) error {

+			return true
+		},
+	}

```

  I would consider using AffAfter with 1s delay day one, if we don't use delay then it becomes trcky to add later.
  
  When the delay is used, then the workqueue deduplicates the Reconcile events, so this is a mechnism for batching "Reconcile" events in time. I'm not clear if we need Reconcile per every event. 
  
  The same comment applies to other places.

```diff
+package multikueue
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"fmt"
+
+	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/equality"
+	apierrors "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
+	apimeta "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/meta"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
+	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/types"
+	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/sets"
+	"k8s.io/client-go/util/workqueue"
+	"k8s.io/utils/ptr"
+	ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/builder"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/controller"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/event"
+	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/handler"

+
```
